The table notes 1 ascension from one village — its terms improve accordingly. Nobody has ever seen that before.
The table notes the overtime: 1 week kept waiting, 3 of Haven lost meanwhile. Every door's standing −13.

◈ THE RELAY — You plug into Haven's switchboard — or deeper, straight into the wires. The other paths used the System as terrain. Here it is the relationship. Push enough buttons, try enough things, and you start to integrate with the matrix. The matrix, it turns out, has been waiting for someone to try.
The table has read your ledger. More promises broken than kept; the protected outnumber the killed; the food truth was shared. The light road is open.
◈ REROUTE — honest. Every message carried faithfully, in order, undistorted. Slow. The wires feel *clean*. Somewhere, the System notes the cleanliness with something like respect.
◈ THE RELAY — the last choice is yours, and it's live.
◈ THE RELAY — "Teach it to let go."
"You don't need me inside you," you tell it. "You need to be *good at this* out here." And it listens — the hardest thing a god can do. The System becomes Earth's champion from a respectful distance: funding, shielding, learning names. You keep your humanity. It keeps your lessons. The wires go quiet, and the quiet is kind.
You learned where to put the light so that a quiet person could be seen. You learned when to turn it off.
You left the people in the picture something that belonged to them when the watching stopped.
◈ THE ARCHITECT — Sofia Chen ascended through doors you built: relay. The galaxy will remember the god's name. The village will remember who built the door. Both are true. Neither is small.
The terms held — the table had priced the village, ascension and all, and the village paid in full.
◈ THE OVERSIGHT —
You won. Say it plainly: you won. The village endures. The table signed. The galaxy applauds, and the applause is real, and you earned it.
And somewhere out there — past the cameras, past the light — in rows of humming glass, the little ones are still napping.
The System put a pod on the screen, and you let the segment play.
They are still there. Still small. Still waiting for someone to decide they matter more than the moment.
That is what the word means. Not the watching — anyone can watch. The oversight is the looking away. The most terrible thing the System ever did, it did by accident, because it was bored. You did it on purpose, because you were busy.
The village will tell your story for years. It will be a good story. Listen for the part they don't tell.
💛 You hold Trail compass. Lost above the fog line, visibility down to your boots. You didn't panic — just held the compass flat, found north, and walked out like it was nothing. It was not nothing. Your gifts are not mastered — but they are lived-in, and they are still yours. The feast was the weapon.
💛 You hold Mother's Ring. The winter the factory closed Sandra pressed this into your hand and said: fix what you can reach. She twisted it when she was thinking, which was always. You twist it now. Your gifts are not mastered — but they are lived-in, and they know your hands. The feast was the weapon.
💛 You hold Tasting Spoon. Your first Saturday service, you were sixteen and the chef handed you this spoon without a word. Tap twice, taste, know. You still tap twice — the tickets are gone but the habit isn't. Your gifts are not mastered — but they are lived-in, and they have carried you this far. The feast was the weapon.
